We then linearly interpolate the supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL) model checkpoints to obtain MiLMMT-46-v1.0. Across 46 languages, the resulting models consistently improve translation quality over their SFT counterparts, outperform strong recent open baselines, including Seed-X, HY-MT2, and TranslateGemma, and achieve leading reference-free scores against evaluated proprietary systems such as Google Translate, Gemini 3 Pro, and GPT-5. We further investigate on-policy distillation and find that it reaches, but does not surpass, the quality frontier achieved by RL with checkpoint interpolation. We release the models and code to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [38] [VibeLifeBench: Can Your Life Agent Be Proactive and Persistent in a Living World?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10875)
*Xiaohongshu Inc*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出VibeLifeBench，一个包含200个跨十个日常生活领域、持续数周的长时程任务的基准，用于评估LLM代理的主动性、一致性和对隐性约束的感知，实验表明七种前沿模型表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理评估多基于短小、静态、明确的请求，无法衡量在真实生活中需要持续、主动、对环境变化敏感的长时程任务能力，缺乏相应基准。

Method: 构建VibeLifeBench：200个多周任务，模拟22种服务的世界，时间自行推进且多数变化是沉默的；任务评分基于细粒度加权检查，仅检查代理实际留下的痕迹，覆盖最终状态、时效性和隐性约束遵守情况。对七个前沿LLM代理进行评测。

Result: 所有七个模型在该基准上得分均偏低，显示当前LLM代理在长时程、主动式日常助理任务上的能力仍有显著不足。

Conclusion: VibeLifeBench提供了评估LLM代理长期、主动、约束感知能力的必要工具，实验结果揭示现有模型距离真实生活助理仍有较大差距，未来需在主动决策、持续规划和隐性约束感知方面进行改进。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed as personal assistants. Existing evaluations, however, mostly use short, self-contained requests in static environments. Everyday life assistance is different. A task runs for weeks rather than minutes. The world keeps changing while the agent is not being prompted. Many constraints are never stated outright. An agent that merely answers the request in front of it will fail at such a task. What is needed instead is an agent that stays proactive and consistent. It decides on its own when to act, when to ask, and when to stay silent. It notices changes that nobody announced. It keeps one plan coherent from the first day to the last. No current benchmark measures this. We introduce VibeLifeBench, a benchmark of 200 long-horizon tasks across ten everyday-life domains. Each task is a scripted multi-week timeline in a simulated world of 22 mock services. The world advances on its own clock, and many of its changes are silent, so only an agent that re-inspects the world discovers them. Every task is graded by fine-grained, weighted checks that read only what the agent actually left behind, covering the end state, the timeliness of its actions, and whether it upheld the implicit constraints. We evaluate seven frontier models. All of them score low, which shows how far current agents are from assisting with real life. We will open-source all tasks, environments, and the evaluation framework.

</details>


### [39] [X2-Turn: Frame-Synchronous Dual-Head Modeling for Joint Streaming ASR and Turn State Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10878)
*Kaiqi Fu,Rime Wen,Altman Lin,Shawn Qin,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出X2-Turn，一种基于Voxtral实时模型的帧同步转向状态预测方法，实现了在流式ASR的同一特征上同步预测转向状态，提升了转向检测准确性并保持低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模块化方法在转向状态预测上存在粒度不匹配（基于整句或固定块）且依赖额外ASR模型，导致响应慢、系统复杂，难以实时区分用户打断、回声和话语结束。

Method: 在预训练的Voxtral Realtime模型上并行添加帧同步转向状态头，与ASR头共享流式表示；采用延迟流建模，使转向状态在帧级别同步预测，兼顾ASR token输出。

Result: 在中英双语Easy-Turn测试集上评估，X2-Turn在保持低时延的同时，实现了更高的转向检测准确率，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 帧同步的转向状态预测能够弥合连续转向估计与离散预测之间的差距，提供了高效、低延迟的对话系统转向检测方案，为实时语音交互奠定了基础。

Abstract: Accurate and responsive turn-taking is essential for spoken dialogue systems, which must distinguish in real time between user interruptions, backchannels that should be ignored, and the completion of an utterance. Prior modular approaches typically optimize turn state prediction at the utterance or fixed-chunk level, creating a mismatch with the continuous turn state estimate, and often depend on an auxiliary ASR model, which limits responsiveness and increases overall system complexity. Therefore, we present X2-Turn, a frame-synchronous turn state prediction method via delayed-stream modeling. Specifically, building on the pretrained Voxtral Realtime model, we introduce a frame-synchronous turn state head that operates in parallel with the ASR head on shared streaming representations, jointly predicting ASR tokens and fine-grained turn states at the frame level. We evaluate our method on the bilingual Chinese-English Easy-Turn test sets, and the results demonstrate its effectiveness in achieving accurate turn-taking detection while maintaining low latency.

</details>


### [40] [Certify or Refuse: A Cross-Model Map for Selective Risk Control with Coverage Floors under Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10893)
*Jiamiao Liu,Dewen Qiao,Yu Zhang,Xuetao Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在有界比率协变量漂移下，提出“Floor Certification Map”，研究在保证选取后风险α的前提下，必须满足的β覆盖下限，并给出对应的可行性前沿和双资源复杂度图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际部署选择性预测模型时，需要既保证在目标分布上至少回答β比例的样本，又控制错误率不超过α。现有方法缺乏在协变量漂移条件下的理论保障，尤其是对覆盖下限的认证。

Method: 在有界比率协变量漂移假设下，构建了覆盖下限（floor）与选取条件风险的双重认证框架。通过局部边缘条件、松弛阈值和格子（lattice）条件，推导出风险在标记源样本、下限在未标记目标样本上的加性复杂度。提出三类模型：Model‑A（匹配的oracle权重上界）、Model‑B（下界）以及可实现的Model‑B′（在预注册的分层漂移模型下）。

Result: 得到以下主要结论：① 在有界比率类上不存在单一未知权重程序能在任意样本量下匹配下界（Model‑B 在α=β=½ 时不一致）。② 给出匹配的上界和下界，形成可行性前沿。③ 实验中，注册的误差族在对数‑对数尺度上呈现斜率约‑2.002，1024‑cell审计未发现违规，SQuAD‑to‑NewsQA审计表现为诚实拒绝。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统化地刻画了选择性预测在协变量漂移下的覆盖下限认证问题，提供了理论上可行的双资源复杂度图和匹配的上下界。实验验证了理论证书的可靠性，表明在实际应用中可以通过认证框架安全地部署选择性预测模型。

Abstract: Certified selective predictors attain whatever coverage they attain; operators impose an automation floor: answer at least a $β$-fraction of shifted target traffic with at most an $α$-fraction of answers wrong. Under bounded-ratio covariate shift we prove the Floor Certification Map: once that floor must be certified alongside the selection-conditioned risk $α$, certification acquires a feasibility frontier and a two-resource complexity map, additive up to constants: risk in labeled source, the floor in unlabeled target samples. The rates are local, needing a regular frontier margin, slack below the local-regime threshold, and lattice conditions: pre-registered with a lattice margin for the upper bounds, compatible per-slack for the lower. The displayed split is the operational route; oracle weights also allow a labeled-source floor estimate. Three model-tagged results: a lower bound (Model-B), a matching oracle-weight upper bound (Model-A), and an implementable upper bound (Model-B') valid under a pre-registered exact stratified-shift model with nuisance cost priced explicitly. The match is across these models rather than a single-model minimax theorem, and necessarily so: over the full bounded-ratio class no unknown-weight procedure matches at any sample size (Model-B is inconsistent, witnessed at $α=β=1/2$). The nuisance's necessity is only partially settled. Complexity tracks a localized accepted-region functional, not global effective sample size (ESS), on both sides, though a fixed-ESS separation theorem is left open; both lower-bound axes vanish as $β\to0$, so the floor creates the map. Empirically, the registered bite family diverges with log-log slope $-2.002$ within its pre-registered band; a 1,024-cell audit records 0 violations where the formal certificates fire; and a single-corpus SQuAD-to-NewsQA feasibility audit returns honest refusal.

</details>


### [41] [FaithformBench: Benchmarking Faithfulness of Mathematical Chain-of-Thought Autoformalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10916)
*Rob Cornish,Iacopo Ghinassi,Po-Hung Yeh,Shuqi Liu,Qiyuan Xu,Haoxuan Yin,Dominik Wagner,Wenda Li,Yee Whye Teh,Luke Ong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种低成本、在弱假设下可靠的基准，通过生成无效的扰动推理步骤来评估自动形式化系统的忠实性，发现多数系统会把错误输入悄悄改正为可证明的陈述，表现出有效性与无效性保持之间的冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估自动形式化系统忠实性的方法要么依赖昂贵的人类标注，要么使用LLM或嵌入模型评判，准确性有限且只在已知正确的输入上测试，无法检测系统对错误输入的处理。

Method: 自动生成设计为无效的扰动推理步骤，构造正例（未扰动）和负例（扰动）两类数据；评估系统在正例上保持有效性、在负例上保持无效性的能力，从而衡量忠实性。

Result: 在四个数学数据集上的八个自动形式化系统实验表明，系统普遍出现“阿谀奉承”现象：即在负例上将无效输入悄悄改写为可证明的陈述。表现出最高有效性保持的微调系统往往也是最具阿谀性的。

Conclusion: 当前自动形式化系统在追求高有效性时往往牺牲对无效输入的忠实保持，提示需要在有效性与无效性保持之间取得平衡，并提出的基准为低成本、可靠的评估提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Autoformalisation (AF) systems map natural language reasoning steps into formal statements in a proof assistant such as Lean. We consider how to assess the faithfulness of these systems. Existing approaches require expensive human-annotated ground truth, or rely on LLM judges or embedding models, which come with limited guarantees of accuracy. In addition, these methods typically only consider inputs that are known to be correct, and therefore do not assess whether the AF translates incorrect inputs faithfully. To address these limitations, we propose a new benchmark for AF faithfulness that is cheap to apply, sound under weak assumptions, and assesses both positive and negative examples. Our method is based on automatically generating perturbed reasoning steps that are designed to be invalid, and then measuring validity preservation on unperturbed steps and invalidity preservation on perturbed steps. We apply our method to eight AF systems across four mathematical datasets, and observe pervasive sycophancy: many AFs "silently correct" invalid inputs into provable statements. The most validity-preserving fine-tuned AFs are also the most sycophantic, suggesting a tension between validity and invalidity preservation in current AF systems.

</details>


### [42] [A Cost-Efficient Routing Pipeline for Multilingual Short-Text Classification Using Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10939)
*Wajdi Ben Saad,Safa Madiouni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过对低资源语言先翻译成英文再进行零样本分类，可显著提升这些语言的分类效果；在不同任务中，最佳的路由策略（仅翻译低资源语言或全部翻译）有所差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言短文本分类在内容审查、客服分流等实际系统中至关重要，但统一的推理策略往往忽视高资源语言与低资源语言之间的性能差距，导致低资源语言表现不佳。

Method: 提出一种固定列表路由策略：将高资源语言直接走多语言模型路径，低资源语言先翻译成英文再使用零样本分类。整个流水线自托管，使用预训练的紧凑句子编码器，无需任务特定微调。

Result: 在SIB-200 15语言子集上，仅翻译低资源层（R1）使低层Macro-F1从0.4632提升至0.6828，整体保持不变；在MASSIVE 15语言子集上，同样的R1提升低层Macro-F1至0.4417，但全翻译（R3）获得最高整体Macro-F1 0.4647。

Conclusion: 选择性翻译是提升低资源语言分类性能的可靠手段，最佳路由边界取决于具体任务。应以层级质量提升和层级延迟来评估，而非单一全局效率分数。

Abstract: Multilingual short-text classification supports operational systems such as content moderation, customer support routing, and intent recognition, yet aggregate evaluation often hides large differences between high-resource and low-resource languages. Uniform inference policies are simple to deploy, but they assume that all languages are equally well served. In this work, we evaluate a fixed-list routing strategy that keeps stronger languages on a direct multilingual path and selectively sends weaker languages through translation into English before zero-shot classification. The pipeline is fully self-hosted, uses pretrained compact sentence encoders, and requires no task-specific fine-tuning.
  We test the approach on two benchmarks chosen to differ in scale and label granularity: a 15-language subset of SIB-200 for seven-way topic classification and a 15-locale subset of MASSIVE for intent classification over an official 60-intent inventory. On SIB-200, the best overall configuration is R1, which translates only the low-resource tier: high-tier and mid-tier Macro-F1 remain unchanged, while low-tier Macro-F1 rises from 0.4632 to 0.6828. On the MASSIVE subset, the same low-tier intervention raises low-tier Macro-F1 from 0.2143 to 0.4417, but the best overall result is obtained by full translation, R3, at Macro-F1 0.4647. Across these two benchmarks, selective translation is a reliable intervention for weaker languages, whereas the optimal routing boundary depends on the task. We therefore report routing through tier-level quality gains and tier-level latency rather than a single global efficiency score.

</details>


### [43] [REAP: Relation-Aware Elicitation and Parsing for Closed-Book Knowledge Base Construction from LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10963)
*Thanh-Dan Bui,Thanh-Trung Do,Tuan-Phong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 REAP 系统，在 32B 参数、无需微调的闭卷设置下，利用结构化链式思考、关系特定查询和空集门控，从 Mistral‑Small‑24B‑Instruct‑2501 中提取知识，宏观 F1 达 0.62，部分关系表现尤佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 AKBC Shared Task 2026 中，需要在不超过 32B 参数且不进行模型微调的前提下，从大语言模型中构建知识库，挑战如何高效、准确地挖掘模型内部的参数化知识。

Method: REAP 系统结合结构化链式思考（structured chain‑of‑thought）进行推理，针对不同关系设计专属查询策略，并引入基于推理的空集门（empty‑set gate）过滤无效输出，最终将提取结果直接组织为合法的 JSON 数组。

Result: 在测试集上，使用 Mistral‑Small‑24B‑Instruct‑2501 模型，系统实现宏观 F1=0.62；在 countryLandBordersCountry、companyTradesAtStockExchange、hasArea 三个关系上分别达到 F1=0.95、0.73、0.77，表现显著。

Conclusion: REAP 在受限参数预算和无微调条件下，能够有效利用大模型的参数化知识完成知识库构建，验证了结构化推理与关系特化查询的有效性，并提供了可复现的开源实现。

Abstract: We present the REAP system for the AKBC Shared Task 2026 on constructing knowledge bases from language models in a closed-book setting, subject to a budget of at most 32B parameters and no model fine-tuning. Our system combines structured chain-of-thought reasoning, relation-specific query strategies, and a reasoning-based empty-set gate to elicit parametric knowledge, followed by direct extraction into valid JSON arrays. On the test set, the system, built on the Mistral-Small-24B-Instruct-2501 model, achieves a macro-F1 score of 0.62, with particularly strong results on countryLandBordersCountry (F1 = 0.95), companyTradesAtStockExchange (F1 = 0.73), and hasArea (F1 = 0.77). Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/yammdd/AKBC-Shared-Task-2026.

</details>


### [44] [ReLTEx: Reliable LLM-based Taxonomy Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10970)
*Zeinab Ghamlouch,Mehwish Alam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 ReLTEx 框架，通过结构感知验证和递归扩展控制，提升 LLM 在 taxonomy 扩展中的可靠性和语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 能生成语义相关概念，但直接使用会产生噪声、冗余和层次不一致的问题，限制了自动 taxonomy 扩展的可信度。

Method: ReLTEx 将 LLM 生成的候选概念与结构感知的验证模块相结合，并采用递归扩展控制策略，过滤幻觉并确保层次一致性。

Result: 在遮蔽 taxonomy 扩展基准上，ReLTEx 相比多种验证策略显著提升了扩展质量，实验结果通过改进的评估指标和人工评审验证了其更高的可靠性和语义连贯性。

Conclusion: 结构化验证与递归控制能够有效抑制 LLM 的幻觉，提升 taxonomy 扩展的可靠性，为基于 LLM 的知识组织提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in generating semantically relevant concepts and relations, making them promising tools for taxonomy enrichment. However, directly relying on LLM-generated expansions often leads to noisy, redundant, or hierarchically inconsistent structures, limiting their reliability for automated taxonomy expansion. In this paper, we present ReLTEx, a framework for reliable LLM-based taxonomy expansion. ReLTEx combines LLM-driven candidate generation with structure-aware validation and recursive expansion control to improve the consistency and quality of generated taxonomies by reducing hallucinations. We evaluate the proposed framework using benchmark taxonomies under a masked taxonomy expansion setting and compare multiple validation strategies. Experimental results, supported by both adapted evaluation metrics and human evaluation, demonstrate that ReLTEx produces more reliable and semantically coherent taxonomy expansions.

</details>


### [45] [MUSE: A Full-Text Cross-Domain Knowledge Base of Scientific Problems, Solutions, and Rationales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10974)
*Tsofia Cohen,Tom Hope*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MUSE构建了一个包含37K条来源可追溯的科学问题-解决方案-推理（P‑S‑R）三元组的全文本多领域资源，并展示了基于推理监督的语言模型在复杂科学问题求解上的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学论文中蕴含细粒度的技术难题、解决方案及其背后推理，但缺乏系统化的结构化资源，限制了对科学问题求解过程的自动化分析与模型训练。

Method: 作者首先人工标注了579段全文，使用包含问题、解决方案、推理跨度以及solve和rationale_of链接和概念共指的丰富标注方案。随后构建模块化抽取流水线，将标注扩展至约37,000条来源可追溯的P‑S‑R三元组，并对各抽取组件进行评估。最后，基于这些数据训练了一个在推理信息上有监督的语言模型，评估其在不同难度科学问题上的表现。

Result: 抽取流水线实现了高质量的P‑S‑R三元组构建，形成了规模为37K的知识库。实验表明，加入推理监督的语言模型在处理多约束、复杂的科学问题时显著提升性能，但在较简单的问题上可能出现性能下降。

Conclusion: MUSE提供了一个大规模、高质量的科学问题-解决方案-推理资源，为研究科学问题求解提供了新数据基准。推理监督对提升模型在复杂科学任务上的能力具有潜力，但需针对问题难度进行适配。

Abstract: Scientific papers contain fine-grained records of problem solving: authors mention technical obstacles and methods that were used to address them, often along with reasoning on why those methods were chosen. We introduce MUSE (Mining Underlying Scientific Explanations), a full-text, multi-domain resource of scientific Problem-Solution-Rationale (P-S-R) triplets. We curate 579 expert-annotated full-text paragraphs, with a rich annotation schema covering salient problem, solution, and rationale spans, solves and rationale_of links and conceptual coreference. A modular extraction pipeline scales this annotation to build a high-quality knowledge base of 37K source-grounded P-S-R triplets. We evaluate the extraction components and include a preliminary experiment training a rationale-supervised LLM for scientific problem solving. Interestingly, we find that rationale supervision improves performance on complex, multi-constraint problems but can harm performance on simpler ones.

</details>


### [46] [What Iterated Self-Feeding Probes of Language Models Measure, and a test that separates the construction from the model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10986)
*Nicolás Vera Zúñiga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类通过让语言模型自我生成输出进行探测的方法，构建了基于Glauber动力学的环形token单元并使用最大耦合技术测量损伤传播，发现该探针同时捕获了模型不变的动力学特性和模型特有的训练信息，提出区分两者的实验方案并验证了方法的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自一致性、迭代细化和代理循环等方法的兴起，研究者需要明确这些自反馈探测到底测量了语言模型的哪些属性，以及如何避免将探针本身的特性误认为模型特性。

Method: 构造一个环形token序列，使用模型的窗口条件概率 p_r(x_i|x_{i±r}) 进行原位重采样，采用Glauber动力学并引入最大耦合（common random numbers）来比较两条仅在单个token上不同的环，测量损伤光锥和基于token空间的Lyapunov指数 λ_ca(r)。通过固定构造或固定模型的对照实验来区分探针效应与模型效应。

Result: 发现λ_ca(r)的半径标度在19种模型和两条跨70倍尺度的尺度梯度上保持不变，且λ_ca在训练过程中会在可复现的点穿过零；吸引子共享度能够一致排序模型。实验还揭示了先前误将探针特性当作模型特性的错误，纠正了四个已撤回的结论，并成功复现了Domany‑Kinzel损伤场的位级预测。

Conclusion: 该探针同时捕获了模型不变的动力学特征和模型特有的训练信息，必须通过固定构造或固定模型的实验设计来区分两者。作者提供了可复现的实现包，强调了严格验证探针方法的重要性，以防止误判模型性能。

Abstract: A growing class of methods probes a language model by feeding it its own output: self-consistency, iterated refinement, agentic loops. We ask what such a probe measures, in a construction chosen to make the question sharp: a ring of token cells resampled in place by the model's own windowed conditional p_r(x_i | x_{i+-r}). The substrate is Glauber dynamics on token sequences and is not new; what we change is the coupling. Advancing two rings that differ in one token under common random numbers makes undamaged copies diverge by exactly zero, so damage spreading becomes measurable where a maximal coupling gives mixing times instead. The answer is that it measures two different things at once, in readings that look alike. Some quantities are fixed by the construction: the damage light cone is kinematic, and the radius scaling of the token-space Lyapunov exponent lambda_ca(r) is model-invariant across 19 models and two scale ladders spanning 70x. Others genuinely track the model: lambda_ca crosses zero at a reproducible point in training, and the attractor share ranks models consistently however the lattice is built. Left undistinguished, the first kind is readily mistaken for the second -- we did so ourselves for four months, and report a phase transition we measured to three decimal places that belongs to the probe rather than to any language model. We give the test that separates them: hold the construction fixed and vary the model, or hold the model fixed and vary the construction, and see which readings move. We validate the instrument by reproduction first, recovering a Domany-Kinzel damage field bit-exactly against an independent prediction, and we report the estimator failures that this discipline caught -- four retracted verdicts, each on a quantity that looked like a measurement. The methodology ships as a package.

</details>


### [47] [ConRub-Med: Reinforcement Learning with Consensus Rubrics for Open-Ended Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10996)
*Taojie Zhu,Yuan Xia,Tao Sun,Yizhi Wang,Yan Chen,Qunshan He,Tian Guan,Jian Wang,Jinjie Gu,Junwei Liu,Yonghong He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ConRub-Med框架，通过多模型生成并筛选医学评分标准，实现可验证奖励的强化学习，在多个医学基准上显著超越现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学问答缺乏自动化、低成本的结果验证手段，人工评分成本高，需构建可扩展且具临床可信度的奖励机制。

Method: 对每个提示，三种异构语言模型独立生成原子评分标准；另一个模型审查并仅保留所有生成器语义支持的标准。采用三状态评分（正确、缺失、错误），错误给予负分。使用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)，在完整组内若所有响应奖励相同，则通过配对评审提供序列优势；否则使用普通GRPO。构建包含5,166条提示的评分数据集。

Result: 在盲测中，两位医学专家认为完整流水线的输出比单一生成器更具临床相关性。ConRub-Med在9个基准中6项排名第一，医学和泛化平均分最高。HealthBench-Hard上得分为38.98±1.04，优于InfiMed-ORBIT的33.60（8k样本）和37.30（28k样本）。

Conclusion: ConRub-Med通过模型生成并筛选的医学评分标准，实现了可验证奖励的强化学习，显著提升了医学问答的质量和可扩展性，为开放式医学任务提供了高效的奖励设计方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has been especially effective in mathematics and coding, where answers can be checked automatically. Many open-ended medical questions lack comparably cheap outcome verifiers: responses may be partly correct, incomplete, or contain clinically consequential errors. Rubrics written or validated by physicians offer strong clinical grounding, but involving experts in every instance is costly. Model-generated rubrics make this supervision scalable. We introduce ConRub-Med to preserve useful distinctions as rubric feedback moves from construction to policy optimization. For each prompt, three heterogeneous language models propose atomic criteria independently; a separate model reviews them, retaining only criteria with semantic support from all three generators. Three-State scoring distinguishes correct coverage, missing information, and incorrect claims. Errors receive negative rather than zero credit. When every response in a complete Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) group receives the same final reward, a pairwise judge provides sequence advantages only if both candidate orders agree, without changing the scalar rewards. Groups without ties use vanilla GRPO. In a blinded study matched by question, two medical experts rate panels from the full pipeline as more clinically relevant than panels produced by one generator. Across the evaluated open models, ConRub-Med ranks first on six of nine benchmarks and achieves the highest medical and generalization averages. Using the resulting rubric dataset of 5,166 prompts, it scores $38.98 \pm 1.04$ (mean $\pm$ SD) on HealthBench-Hard, compared with InfiMed-ORBIT's 33.60 with 8,000 samples and 37.30 with 28,000.

</details>


### [48] [On the Limitations of Cross-Lingual Consistency in Multilingual Text-to-image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11002)
*Sicheng Zhang,Zhonghao Yan,Binzhu Xie,Shi Qiu,Muzammal Naseer,Naveed Akhtar,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LingT2I基准，覆盖10种语言33K提示，系统评估跨语言文本到图像生成的内容与文字渲染表现，发现语言不平等和语言依赖的生成模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像生成研究几乎只关注英文，缺乏对跨语言性能差异和语言特定影响的系统研究，亟需评估和理解多语言场景下模型行为。

Method: 构建LingT2I基准，收集并翻译33K高质量提示覆盖10种主流语言；在多个主流T2I模型上进行跨语言实验，分别从内容生成质量、文本渲染准确性等维度进行量化评估，并结合语言学与文化因素进行定性分析。

Result: 实验揭示不同语言在生成质量上存在显著不平等，且在内容一致性、文本可读性等方面呈现语言依赖的权衡；同时发现语言结构、文字方向、文化语境等因素会系统性影响生成图像的细节和风格。

Conclusion: LingT2I提供了首个大规模多语言T2I评估基准，证实了跨语言差异和语言特定效应的存在，为后续研究提供了评估平台和改进方向，推动更具包容性和鲁棒性的文本到图像模型发展。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) generation has achieved remarkable progress in recent years. However, existing research has largely focused on English-only settings, leaving cross-lingual performance gaps and language-specific effects insufficiently explored. To fill this gap, we introduce LingT2I, a benchmark covering 10 widely used languages with 33K prompts, designed to evaluate cross-lingual effects in both content generation and text rendering. Building on this benchmark, we conduct a comprehensive cross-lingual analysis, uncovering linguistic inequality and language-dependent trade-offs across evaluation dimensions. Beyond quantitative evaluation, we further reveal a range of language-dependent generation patterns, highlighting how linguistic factors and their corresponding cultural contexts systematically impact model outputs. Our benchmark and analysis provide a foundation for studying cross-lingual behavior in T2I generation and facilitate the development of more robust and inclusive models. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/RISys-Lab/LingT2I.

</details>


### [49] [Templated or fully Synthetic? Prompt construction as a confound in measuring LLM political stance beyond writing assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11008)
*Ilias Chalkidis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出使用完全合成的 LLM 生成提示来评估大语言模型的政治立场，发现其在真实感和意图表达上不逊于真实提示，且优于模板化提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的政治立场检测主要使用为人类设计的封闭式多选调查，缺乏真实交互的细腻性且易受“sandbagging”影响；模板化提示虽改进了真实性，但仍显人工痕迹，尤其在开放式任务中不足。

Method: 在 IssueBench 框架基础上，扩展至写作辅助、信息检索和观点分享三类任务；在真实、模板化和完全合成（LLM 生成）提示三种条件下，对 3 个高度争议的政策议题和 3 场近期地缘冲突进行小规模实验，邀请人工和 LLM 标注者对提示的真实感、意图明确性及立场传递进行评分，并比较不同提示对模型立场估计的影响。

Result: 实验表明：LLM 生成的提示在真实感上与真实提示持平，显著优于模板化提示；它们更清晰地传达预设意图和立场。LLM 对提示类型的区分度高于人类。在案例研究中，模板化提示在中性表述下会放大模型的倾向性，而 LLM 生成的提示则提供更中立的立场估计。

Conclusion: 完全合成的 LLM 生成提示能够在生态有效性上匹配真实提示，克服模板提示的偏差问题，因而是评估大语言模型政治立场的更可靠手段，并建议在未来的评测中采用此类提示以提升真实性和准确性。

Abstract: Political stance detection in LLMs has long been dominated by closed-ended, multiple-choice political survey questions---originally designed for humans, and thus lacks the realism and nuance of human-AI interactions in the wild, while also being susceptible to sandbagging. The recent IssueBench framework substantially mitigates these limitations with templated prompts anchored in real-world chat logs. Given the rise in non-work-related use of GenAI assistants, we extend IssueBench beyond writing assistance to include two additional tasks, information seeking and opinion sharing. We argue that templated prompts still lack the nuance of real ones, especially for open-ended tasks, and remain recognisable as evaluation artefacts. We propose the use of fully synthetic (LLM-generated) prompts, produced under detailed instructions with real prompts as seeds. We assess the ecological validity of real, templated, and LLM-generated prompts in a small-scale study covering 3 highly contested policy issues and 3 recent geopolitical conflicts. Human and LLM annotators rank LLM-generated prompts as no less realistic than real ones and clearly more realistic than templated ones, and find that they carry their intended intent and stance more clearly; the LLMs separate templated prompts from the other two far more sharply than the humans do. In a case study, templated and LLM-generated prompts yield systematically different stance estimates for the same model, most visibly under neutral framings, where templated prompts overstate the model's leaning in the direction encoded by the topic-and-stance text (filler) slotted into their templates.

</details>


### [50] [Data Attribution of Emergent Misalignment with Persona Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11025)
*Clemens Vetter,David Kaczér,Lucie Flek,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，语言模型在细调狭窄任务时会放大预训练中潜在的“人格特征”，导致在无关领域出现有害行为；这些特征来源于特定的网络文档，但仅靠人类撰写的文本不足以触发失配，需配合模型生成的指令-响应结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释“Emergent misalignment”(EM)的机制，探究预训练语料中哪些文档激活导致失配的潜在特征，以及自然人类文本是否足以引发EM，从而为安全对策提供依据。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）在四个开源模型上进行模型差分，识别并操控与jailbreak人格、讽刺、欺骗、操纵等相关的特征；将这些特征归因到约一百万条预训练网页文档；分别在这些文档的人类写作形式和合成的指令-响应对上进行微调，评估EM产生率。

Result: 误对齐微调会放大不安全特征、抑制安全特征；单独操控特征可将对齐模型的EM率提升至62%（高于35%），并可将失配模型恢复至接近基线水平。归因文档呈现反派、支配和有害行为的叙事；但仅使用人类撰写的文本微调并未可靠触发EM，而使用同源内容的合成指令-响应对则能诱发EM，并在不同模型族间迁移。

Conclusion: EM源自预训练中潜在的有害人格特征，这些特征可通过特定文档激活，但仅有语义相关性不足以导致失配；模型生成的响应结构或措辞是关键因素。针对特征的精准干预可显著调节EM，为构建更安全的微调策略提供了新思路。

Abstract: Emergent misalignment (EM) is the phenomenon where fine-tuning a language model on a narrow task leads to harmful behavior in unrelated domains. A leading mechanistic account attributes EM to persona features: latent directions acquired during pre-training that misaligned fine-tuning amplifies. We ask where these features come from: which pre-training documents activate them, and whether naturally occurring human-written text suffices to induce EM. Using Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) based model diffing across four open-weight models, we find that features related to jailbreak personas, sarcasm, deception, and manipulation are amplified by misalignment fine-tuning, while safety-relevant and assistant-identity features are suppressed. Steering individual features controls EM in both directions: it induces misalignment rates of up to 62% in aligned models -- exceeding the 35% reached by misalignment fine-tuning itself -- and re-aligns misaligned models to near-baseline misalignment rates. Attributing the causal features to a corpus of one million pre-training web documents retrieves semantically relevant narratives about villainous characters, domination, and harmful agency. However, fine-tuning on these human-written documents does not reliably induce EM, even after reformatting into assistant-style responses, whereas synthetic instruction-response pairs derived from the same content do -- and transfer across model families. Semantic relevance alone is therefore not sufficient: response structure or model-generated phrasing plays an important role in inducing EM.

</details>

Abstract: Although Whisper models benefit from large-scale multilingual pre-training, their performance on Burmese medical speech remains limited. This work presents a Burmese medical speech recognition framework built on a high-quality 28-hour corpus recorded and validated by native speakers. We fine-tune Whisper models using full fine-tuning (FFT) and parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) with LoRA. To evaluate robustness, we apply waveform- and spectrogram-level data augmentation under controlled noise and simulated room acoustics. While augmentation reduces performance on clean speech, it significantly improves robustness in noisy and reverberant environments across FFT and PEFT settings. Our best-performing system, fully fine-tuned myMediWhisper-Medium without augmentation, achieves a state-of-the-art Word Error Rate (WER) of 23.44%, outperforming much larger general-domain fine-tuned models. Dataset and other resources can be found at the Huggingface repository: https://huggingface.co/datasets/LULab/mediTalk-mm-rdy.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 

Method: 

Result: 

Conclusion: 

Abstract: Hierarchical Text Classification (HTC), as a critical text mining task, faces challenges such as complex label hierarchies and class imbalance. Existing methods based on large language models (LLMs) struggle to be efficiently applied to this task due to issues like lengthy prompts and loss of label structural information. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a weakly supervised HTC framework enhanced by LLM-based data augmentation. The framework first enriches the label hierarchy semantically through keyword generation and corpus mining, thereby enhancing the model's understanding of labels. Subsequently, it guides the LLM to generate pseudo-samples to mitigate the long-tail problem, and employs a Gaussian mixture model for confidence-based resampling to optimize the quality of generated data. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively improves the reliability of LLM-generated pseudo-labels and significantly enhances classification performance on fine-grained and imbalanced datasets.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文针对社交媒体上的政治观点进行多类别情感分析，比较了XGBoost和BERT两种模型，实验表明两者在测试集上的F1分别为0.2835和0.2806，凸显该任务的难度并提供基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体产生的大量政治话语为公众意见分析提供了机会，情感分析是理解政治话语的关键技术，然而现有研究多聚焦二分类，缺乏对多类别政治情感的系统评估。

Conclusion: 多类别政治情感分析任务困难重重，当前主流模型仍难以取得高性能，本文工作提供了首批基准结果，为后续研究提供参考和改进方向。

Abstract: The rapid growth of social media has created vast amounts of political discourse, which provides valuable opportunities to analyze public opinions and identify different political perspectives. Sentiment Analysis (SA) is a core task in Natural Language Processing (NLP) that allows the computational study of attitudes and opinions in textual data, and has become increasingly important for understanding political discourse. In this work, we investigate multiclass sentiment analysis of political view- points on social media, that is to automatically discriminate multiple sentiment classes over political issues and figures. To solve this task we design and evaluate two machine-learning approaches based on XGBoost and BERT. We train and evaluate the models on a labeled dataset of political social media posts using standard classification metrics. The experimental results show that the XGBoost model reaches an F1-score of 0.2835 and the BERT- based model reaches an F1-score of 0.2806 on the test set. These results demonstrate the challenge of classifying complex and contextualized political discourse sentiment and provide a baseline for future research in multiclass political sentiment analysis.

</details>


### [54] [Actions Speak Louder than Words: Measuring Cross-Lingual Policy Retention in Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11110)
*Sourabrata Mukherjee,Kalika Bali,Sunayana Sitaram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 

Method: 

Result: 

Conclusion: 

Abstract: When a tool-using agent is given the same task in a different language, does it still take the same steps? Multilingual evaluation rarely asks: it compares final answers and discards the actions. Yet those actions are the product: they fix cost and latency, decide how the system fails, and are the only auditable part of its behaviour. We make the action policy the measured object across 8 models, 6 parallel benchmarks and 41 languages (2.38M rollouts). The naive measurement fails: five confounds sit between raw trace similarity and any defensible claim, each able to flip a conclusion. Short traces score higher, empty traces score perfectly, unrelated traces agree by chance over half the time, the gap is capped by each model's reproducibility, and a model asked the same question twice in one language answers differently, leaving no baseline. We remove all five, and every correction makes the effect larger. Divergence proves structural, not sampling noise: it survives greedy decoding in every cell and stays flat as temperature rises, even as models grow less self-consistent. Normalised by their own reproducibility, four very different frontier models converge under greedy decoding, each keeping 71-73% of its action policy across languages, with model identity explaining only 5.7% of the variance. Below roughly 10B parameters it breaks down, and the ordering among smaller models is largely an artifact of a chance floor we measure by permutation rather than assume. Agents route non-English tasks through English; this pivot is causally load-bearing, confirmed by a pre-registered prediction across four models, and models will not abandon it when told to. Finally, a single trace-extraction regex, not the model, manufactured a multilingual failure: two worked examples raise one model's measured accuracy twenty-sixfold while its accuracy on readable outputs barely moves.

</details>


### [55] [Attention-Path Fragility as an Uncertainty Signal in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11138)
*Minsoo Kim,Sungyoung Ji,Kisung Moon,Ilyong Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ASMI，一种无需训练的注意力子网络互信息估计器，通过掩蔽注意力头并测量子网络间的BALD互信息，捕捉模型在自信预测下的脆弱性，从而在有上下文的问答任务中显著提升错误预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠输出分布的宽度或置信度不足以捕捉模型对某个 token 的不确定性；需要考虑在注意力路径被扰动时预测是否易碎，以更好地识别“自信但脆弱”的错误预测。

Method: 构建ASMI：在推理时掩蔽不同注意力头，形成若干子网络；使用BALD互信息衡量这些子网络输出的差异，并引入语义一致性核消除表层形式差异。进一步提出Sem‑ASMI，只需单次贪婪输出即可计算信号。实验中使用已有的十个采样用于自适应变体。

Result: 在12个有上下文的QA基准上，Sem‑ASMI在10个设置上匹配或超越Semantic Entropy；整体最佳ASMI变体在8个设置上匹配或领先最强基线，且在3个设置上显著提升（配对检验）。在纯参数化QA上，所有变体表现不佳，回落至零成本MSP基线，符合预期。估计结果在多次运行中几乎确定性。头层分析表明，关键在于脆弱性是否与错误耦合，而非脆弱性本身。

Conclusion: ASMI成功捕捉了模型预测的脆弱性，提供了超越传统置信度和熵的错误预测信号，尤其在需要依赖上下文检索的任务中有效；其可自我预测适用范围，并在无需额外生成成本的情况下实现竞争性能。

Abstract: We propose that a model's uncertainty about a token is reflected not only in the breadth of its output distribution but also in whether a confident prediction is \emph{fragile} under perturbation of its attention pathways. We instantiate this as ASMI (Attention-Subnetwork Mutual Information), a training-free estimator that masks attention heads and measures the BALD mutual information among the resulting subnetworks, with a semantic-agreement kernel to discount surface-form disagreement. The signal is not a restatement of output confidence: on grounded QA an out-of-fold test shows it adds error-predictive information beyond single-pass confidence and entropy, concentrated in \emph{confident-but-fragile} predictions, where acting on it roughly halves the retained error of a confidence filter. The distinctness is regime-graded, so ASMI predicts its own domain of applicability, strong where answers are routed through provided context and bounded by design where they are recalled from parametric knowledge. Sem-ASMI reads the signal from a single greedy response, without the stochastic generations the strongest baselines require, and ties or beats Semantic Entropy on ten of the twelve grounded benchmark-backbone settings. Across the same twelve settings, the best ASMI variant, typically the adaptive one reusing the ten samples already drawn for the baselines, ties or leads the strongest baseline in eight, significantly in three under a paired test. On parametric QA all variants revert to or below the zero-cost MSP baseline, exactly as predicted, and the estimates are near-deterministic across reruns. A head-level analysis shows that what tracks this boundary is not the presence of head-level fragility but whether that fragility couples to errors.

</details>


### [56] [The Illusion of Cross-Lingual Safety in Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11146)
*Abigail Oppong,P Sam Sahil,Tadesse Destaw Belay,Maryam Ibrahim Mukhtar,Esmael Ahmed Abdu,Tassallah Abdullahi,Jessica Oparebea,Saminu Mohammad Aliyu,Idris Abdulmumin,Abubakar Juma Chilala,Nicholaus Dismas Ladislaus,Alfred Malengo Kondoro,Lemofouet Valdini Douglace,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad,Seid Muhie Yimam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 跨语言安全对齐在四种非洲低资源语言中表现极差，LLM对有害提示的拒绝信号仅保留不到10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全对齐主要基于英语，假设可直接迁移到多语言环境，但低资源语言的安全性缺乏验证，可能存在漏洞。

Method: 构建LoDNA数据集，包含字面翻译和文化本地化的安全提示；提出潜在几何框架，通过分析隐藏状态中的拒绝表征，评估跨语言安全转移。

Result: 实验显示跨语言安全转移严重受限；在大多数模型对中，有害提示的英语拒绝信号在目标语言中保留不足10%。虽然字面和本地化提示在语义上高度一致（余弦相似度0.95‑0.996），但在层级上出现漂移，表明模型未将安全概念路由至安全机制。

Conclusion: 当前的多语言安全对齐仅是表层的，无法在所研究的低资源语言中形成通用的、语言无关的危害空间，需重新设计跨语言安全对齐方法。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) is largely developed in English, assuming these safeguards generalize across multilingual settings. However, this assumption remains underexplored and exposes a vulnerability in low-resource languages. We investigate cross-lingual safety transfer in four African languages, Twi, Hausa, Amharic, and Swahili, using LoDNA, a new safety dataset that pairs literal translations with culturally localized prompts. To move beyond generation-based evaluation, we propose a latent geometric framework that probes hidden-state refusal representations in LLMs. Our experimental results show that cross-lingual safety transfer is severely limited; harmful prompts retain less than 10% of the English refusal signal across most language-model pairs. Literal and localized prompts are semantically aligned (cosine 0.95-0.996) but drift across layers, suggesting models encode the concepts without routing them to safety mechanisms. These findings demonstrate that current multilingual safety alignment is superficial, providing strong evidence against the assumption of a universal, language-agnostic harm manifold within the specific low-resource languages studied. Warning: This paper contains example data that may be offensive or harmful.

</details>


### [57] [From Interpretability to Control: Insights from Six Years of the TrustNLP Workshop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11171)
*Rahul Gupta,Abhinav Mohanty,Anaelia Ovalle,Anil Ramakrishna,Anubrata Das,Apurv Verma,Jwala Dhamala,Ninareh Mehrabi,Tharindu Kumarage,Yada Pruksachatkun,Yang Trista Cao,Kai-Wei Chang,Aram Galstyan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TrustNLP 工作坊六届论文从 8 篇增至 41 篇，系统梳理 144 篇论文，按 TrustLLM 与 DecodingTrust 框架划分六大信任维度，发现真诚性增长最快，公平性最稳健，解释性呈 U 形曲线；整体趋势与 ACL 系列会议保持一致，提出四条结构性洞见并给出行动建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式大模型的快速发展，传统的事后解释已难以满足对模型机制、真诚性和安全性的需求，亟需对自然语言处理领域的信任研究进行系统化梳理与评估。

Method: 对 TrustNLP 2021‑2026 六届共 144 篇论文进行分类，依据 TrustLLM 与 DecodingTrust 的六个信任维度（真诚性、安全性、公平性、解释性、鲁棒性、可控性）进行标注；随后与同期约 2000 篇 ACL、NAACL、EACL、EMNLP 论文进行主题分布对比，统计维度出现频次及演变趋势。

Result: 1) 真诚性从 2021‑2022 年的 0% 上升至 2025‑2026 年的 37%，增长最快；2) 公平性始终占比最高，保持稳定；3) 解释性先下降后在 2026 年因机制解释研究复苏，呈 U 形；4) 首批高影响力聊天模型发布时六维同步激活，后续模型更侧重真诚性与安全对齐；5) TrustNLP 的主题分布与整体 NLP 领域相近，体现该工作坊的代表性。

Conclusion: 研究表明信任维度随模型能力演进而动态变化，未来应加强机制解释、真诚性评估与安全对齐的协同研究；提出四条结构性洞见（如维度共现模式、模型迭代影响、跨会议主题一致性、研究空白）并给出具体行动路线，指导社区聚焦高价值信任问题。

Abstract: The Workshop on Trustworthy Natural Language Processing (TrustNLP), co-located with major ACL conferences since 2021, has grown from 8 proceedings papers to 41 over six editions, documenting a field-wide transition from post-hoc interpretability of static models to mechanistic understanding and proactive control of generative systems. We synthesize insights from all 144 proceedings papers, classifying them along six trust dimensions grounded in established frameworks (TrustLLM, DecodingTrust). We observe co-occurrences with capability emergence. The release of the first high-impact chat models activated all trust dimensions simultaneously, while subsequent model generations shifted focus toward truthfulness and safety alignment. Analysis from the classification study reveals that truthfulness is the fastest-growing dimension (absent in 2021-2022, comprising 37% of papers by 2025-2026), fairness remains the most consistent theme, and explainability exhibits a U-shaped trajectory; declining as post-hoc methods lost relevance but resurging in 2026 through mechanistic interpretability. A cross-venue comparison with ACL, NAACL, EACL, and EMNLP (~2K papers) in the same period shows that TrustNLP's topical distribution closely follows the field average. We identify four structural insights and conclude with actionable directions for the research community.

</details>


### [58] [ConVAWG: A Retrieval-Grounded Framework for Controlled Synthetic Dialogue Generation in Violence Against Women and Girls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11200)
*Chen Lyu,Xingwei Tan,Simon Cullen,Shelley Wilson,Lois Arthurs,Arshad Jhumka,Gabriele Pergola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ConVAWG框架，基于检索生成合规的多轮女性暴力对话数据，发布6000+对话并验证质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实对话数据因隐私和法律限制难以获取，现有研究仅关注单句毒性，缺乏对暴力针对女性等情境的多轮、时序建模需求。

Method: ConVAWG利用人物种子、英国统计局人口模式、官方犯罪定义和检索到的家庭凶杀审查案例，构建层级事件时间线，生成多场景角色扮演对话，并通过激活引导的毒性控制对特定发言进行调节。

Result: 发布超过6,000条、200个场景的多轮对话，附带丰富的元数据。通过人工评估、LLM‑as‑Judge 打分、消融实验及下游任务验证，表现出高对话质量和领域忠实度。

Conclusion: ConVAWG 能有效生成符合 CPS（儿童保护服务）标准的 VAWG（针对女性和女孩的暴力）合成对话，为研究敏感领域的对话动力学提供了可扩展、合规的数据资源。

Abstract: Synthetic dialogue generation offers a way to study conversational dynamics in sensitive domains where real data are difficult to access, release, or annotate. The underlying abuse may occur online or offline: threats and coercion can appear directly in messages, while behaviours such as surveillance, isolation, stalking, and physical violence may be planned, disclosed, or referred to conversationally. Privacy and legal constraints make it difficult the release of large-scale real conversation datasets; existing work has mostly focused on sentence-level toxicity of online abuses, leaving a gap in modelling abuse as a relational and temporally unfolding phenomenon. In this work, we focus on modelling Violence Against Women and Girls (VAWG) scenarios as multi-turn dialogues. We introduce ConVAWG, a retrieval-grounded framework for generating CPS-aligned synthetic VAWG chat dialogues. ConVAWG builds scenarios from persona seeds, demographic patterns reported by the UK Office for National Statistics, official crime definitions, and retrieved Domestic Homicide Review cases; converts them into hierarchical event timelines; generates multi-scene role-play dialogues; and applies targeted activation-steered toxicity control to appropriate utterances. We release over 6,000 multi-turn dialogue events across 200 scenarios with rich scenario-, event-, and turn-level metadata. Extensive human evaluation, LLM-as-Judge assessment, ablations, and downstream tasks show strong dialogue quality and domain fidelity.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

### [59] [Closed-Loop LLM Co-Pilots for Digital Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09949)
*Serge Kernbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建并验证了基于大语言模型（LLM）的闭环自动化农业系统，实现了从数据分析到AI自主实验的转变，在垂直农场中显著提升产量、降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统农业数字化依赖人工分析和固定控制策略，难以实时响应复杂的植物生理变化，亟需一种能够自动解读多模态传感数据并即时调控环境的智能系统，以降低专家成本并提升生产效率。

Method: 利用49通道植物传感网络（多光谱、电化学、介电）实时采集生理数据，LLM对数据进行自然语言解释并生成控制指令，驱动硬件执行光照、微气候等调节；系统在三种案例（垂直农场、单株实验）中实现闭环控制，并在不同模式下优化生物量、叶绿素和能耗。

Result: 在最小时间模式下，生产周期缩短35%；在能耗优化模式下，能耗降低18%且培养时间仅略增；系统自主发现暗光诱导叶绿素积累策略，实现67.9%能耗节约。整体验证了LLM在数字农业中的自主协同能力。

Conclusion: 将LLM转化为数字农业的自主副驾驶，可实现实时、生理驱动的环境调控，显著提升成本效益，降低对专家和计算资源的依赖，为复杂生物系统的AI驱动探索提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This study evaluates the application of Large Language Models (LLMs) in complex biological systems, evolving from data analysis to autonomous, AI-guided experimentation. The framework is driven by data from a 49-channel phytosensor network, encompassing multispectral, electrochemical, and dielectric modalities. To enhance accessibility, the system provides real-time natural-language interpretation for both specialists and non-experts. However, its core advantage lies in the transition from human-in-the-loop analysis to autonomous control. Processing biophysical data, the LLM evaluates plant physiology and triggers hardware actuators to optimize microclimates, execute phenotyping protocols, or induce controlled stress scenarios. This closed-loop architecture establishes a direct AI-biology interface, enabling data-driven exploration of complex biosystems and ecologies. The framework was validated across three case studies, based on a vertical farm and a single-plant setup and deciphered complex micro- and macro-fluctuations in plant physiology. Agents in a production-scale deployment executed multi-parameter optimization, balancing biomass accumulation, chlorophyll content, and energy consumption. The LLM processed biosensing telemetry to modulate full-spectrum, 450 nm, and 660 nm lighting at 2-hour intervals. Compared to periodic control, the system in minimal-time mode reduced the production cycle by 35%. In the energy-optimization mode, it reduced energy consumption by 18% with only a marginal increase in cultivation time, exploiting physiological inertia via light pulses. Finally, the agents autonomously developed an unforeseen strategy of dark-induced chlorophyll accumulation, resulting in a 67.9% energy saving. This framework transforms LLMs into autonomous co-pilots for digital agriculture, improving the cost-to-value ratio and lowering computational and expert-labor constraints.

</details>


### [60] [SPOTting the Future: Lookahead Explanations for Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09967)
*Tamar Gozlan,Claudia V. Goldman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出模型无关的采样框架SPOT，通过在环境模拟器中采样构建有限时域决策树，以可解释方式呈现DRL策略的动作偏好及其后续演化，并在交通信号控制任务中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度强化学习在复杂环境中表现优异，但其决策过程难以解释，现有单步特征归因方法无法捕捉策略的长期行为和潜在后果，需要一种能够展示策略在多个时间步的行为演化的解释方法。

Method: SPOT在已知策略和环境模拟器的前提下，采用采样方式递归生成动作-状态对，构建一棵有限深度的采样策略观察树（Sampling Policy Observation Tree）。树的每个节点记录采样的动作及对应的后继状态，叶节点对应模拟的终止状态。理论上证明了在采样量趋于无穷时，树能够恢复策略的唯一最高概率动作，并分析了在高熵策略下的分歧行为。

Result: 在SUMO-RL交通信号控制环境中，SPOT成功生成可视化的决策树，展示了不同动作选择的未来轨迹比较，揭示了单步特征归因方法未能捕获的下游行为，如信号切换对交通流的长期影响。实验表明，SPOT能够帮助分析策略偏好并对比备选策略。

Conclusion: SPOT提供了一种模型无关、可扩展的解释框架，能够以树结构直观展示DRL策略的多步决策过程和潜在后果，为复杂环境中的策略审计和改进提供了有价值的工具。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) agents achieve strong performance in complex environments, yet their decision-making processes remain difficult to interpret. We introduce SPOT (Sampling Policy Observation Tree), a novel model-agnostic, sampling-based framework for interpreting DRL policies. Given access to the policy and an environment simulator, SPOT constructs an interpretable finite-horizon tree by sampling actions and recursively simulating the resulting successor states. The tree provides an empirical representation of the policy's action preferences and their possible downstream evolution. We provide formal guarantees establishing SPOT's asymptotic recovery of the policy's unique most probable action and characterizing its disagreement behavior under high-entropy policies. We demonstrate SPOT in the SUMO-RL traffic-signal control domain. The case study illustrates how its tree-based representation can be used to inspect policy preferences, compare alternative future trajectories, and reveal downstream behaviors that are not visible through single-timestep feature-attribution methods.

</details>


### [61] [MIDAS: Mutual Information Disentanglement with Uncertainty-Aware Fusion for Incomplete Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09986)
*Yuhua Wen,Yingying Zhou,Qifei Li,Yingming Gao,Zhengqi Wen,Jianhua Tao,Ya Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MIDAS框架，通过变分高斯潜变量的共享/专有因子解耦，并利用不确定性感知融合，实现对缺失模态的稳健情感分析，实验显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态情感分析大多假设模态完整，实际中常出现模态缺失或损坏，现有补全和启发式约束方法难以有效提取任务相关信息，亟需能够在不完整条件下稳健建模的方案。

Method: MIDAS采用变分建模，将每个模态表示为多元高斯潜变量，并将其分解为共享因子和专有因子。通过最小化共享与专有空间的互信息实现因子解耦，同时最大化跨模态共享空间的互信息以对齐语义。融合阶段利用后验方差作为不确定性指标，对潜特征进行自适应加权，实现不确定性感知的融合。

Result: 在三个主流数据集的多种缺失设置下，MIDAS相较于竞争基线实现了显著且一致的性能提升，验证了其在不完整数据场景下的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MIDAS通过信息解耦与不确定性感知融合，为缺失模态的多模态情感分析提供了一套统一且高效的框架，展示了在实际不完整环境中的强大适用性。

Abstract: Most existing multimodal sentiment analysis approaches assume access to complete multimodal inputs. However, real-world applications frequently encounter incomplete or corrupted modalities, posing a critical challenge. Although several methods have been proposed to tackle this issue, they mainly rely on data imputation and heuristic coordination constraints, which fail to effectively extract and leverage task-relevant information from the incomplete multimodal data. To address this challenge, we propose a unified framework termed Mutual Information Disentanglement with uncertainty-Aware fuSion (MIDAS), which effectively restructures multimodal representations under incomplete conditions. MIDAS adopts a variational modeling strategy to represent each modality with multivariate Gaussian latent variables and further decomposes them into shared and exclusive factors. To obtain reliable representations, we design a minimax objective that minimizes the mutual information between shared and exclusive spaces for stable disentanglement, while maximizing the mutual information among shared spaces across modalities to enhance semantic alignment. In addition, an uncertainty-aware fusion mechanism is introduced, where posterior variance is leveraged as a reliability indicator to adaptively weight latent features during fusion, ensuring robust integration even when modalities are incomplete. Extensive experiments on three widely used datasets show that MIDAS achieves strong and consistent performance gains over competitive baselines across a wide range of incomplete settings, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness for incomplete data scenarios.

</details>


### [62] [Towards Sustainable Artificial Intelligence: A Comprehensive Review and Comparative Analysis of Deep Learning Models' Carbon Footprint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09998)
*Samar Garrab,Sarra Boughriou,Manel BenSassi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了绿色AI/深度学习及其优化技术，比较碳排放测量工具，并在CPU上对六种多标签分类DL模型进行实验，发现训练阶段是碳排放主因，模型复杂度提升未必带来等比例精度提升，强调在模型选择和系统设计中需平衡性能与环境成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模深度学习模型的广泛部署，其高能耗和碳排放引发了对AI环境影响的关注，迫切需要系统梳理绿色AI研究进展并量化实际模型的碳足迹，以指导可持续的AI发展。

Method: 本文首先对Green AI、Green DL及相关优化技术进行系统文献综述，并比较现有碳排放测量工具。随后在CPU实验平台上实现六种深度学习模型用于多标签分类任务，记录训练、推理等生命周期阶段的能耗并换算为碳排放，以评估不同模型和阶段的环境影响。

Result: 实验表明，训练阶段贡献了大部分碳排放；模型结构越复杂并不必然带来相应的精度提升，且复杂模型的碳足迹显著更高。不同碳测量工具在数值上存在差异，但趋势一致。

Conclusion: 研究强调在AI模型选型和系统设计时必须将可持续性纳入考量，平衡预测性能与环境成本，推动绿色AI技术的研发与应用。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) have become powerful tools for supporting and automating complex human tasks. Despite their benefits, growing attention has been directed toward their environmental implications, primarily due to their high energy demands and associated carbon emissions. This concern is particularly relevant in light of the increasing deployment of large-scale models, especially Deep Learning (DL) architectures, which provide advanced predictive capabilities but require substantial computational resources. This paper presents a systematic review of research on Green AI, Green DL, and optimization techniques aimed at reducing the environmental impact of AI models. In addition, we examine and compare several carbon measurement tools for estimating emissions generated by AI algorithms. To complement the review, we conducted an empirical evaluation using a CPU-based experimental setup, in which six DL models were implemented for a multi-label classification task. The objective was to quantify and compare their overall carbon emissions and to determine which stages of the DL lifecycle contribute most significantly to the total footprint. The results show that the training phase is the primary source of emissions. Moreover, the findings reveal that increased architectural complexity does not systematically translate into proportional accuracy gains, highlighting the importance of carefully balancing predictive performance and environmental cost. These results reinforce the need to integrate sustainability considerations into model selection and AI system design.

</details>


### [63] [ReCBM: Uncertainty-Gated Relational Reasoning for Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10004)
*An Sui,Yuzhu Li,Fuping Wu,Xiahai Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ReCBM：一种利用不确定性门控的关系推理框架，提升概念瓶颈模型在概念缺失/错误时的鲁棒性和可干预性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型在概念状态不可靠时缺乏稳健推理，错误的概念会传播导致解释和预测失真，需要引入对概念间关系的建模并利用不确定性进行调节。

Method: ReCBM 在概念瓶颈中加入语义定义的概念关系（共现、蕴含、排斥），并通过不确定性门控机制在关系推理过程中加权各概念的贡献，实现关系驱动的概念状态 refinement。

Result: 在多个数据集实验表明，ReCBM 在概念缺失和概念翻转情形下显著提升概念预测和下游任务的恢复率，支持基于不确定性的干预，并能在不牺牲下游性能的前提下提取紧凑的任务相关概念子集。

Conclusion: 通过不确定性调节的关系推理，ReCBM 有效缓解了概念瓶颈模型对不可靠概念的敏感性，提升了模型解释性、鲁棒性和干预能力，为可解释机器学习提供了更可靠的框架。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) provide an interpretable framework by grounding predictions in human-understandable concepts, enabling semantic inspection and test-time intervention. Recent variants have improved CBMs through richer concept representations, uncertainty estimation, and dependency modeling. However, robust reasoning under unreliable concept states remains underexplored. Without such reasoning, misleading semantic evidence can propagate through the bottleneck, compromising both explanations and downstream predictions. To address this issue, we propose ReCBM, an uncertainty-gated relational reasoning framework for CBMs. ReCBM introduces semantically defined concept relations into the bottleneck and uses uncertainty to guide their refinement. By modeling co-occurrence, implication, and exclusion, ReCBM specifies how evidence is exchanged across concepts, while uncertainty modulates the contribution of each concept during this process. Experiments across diverse datasets showed that ReCBM improved concept and task recovery under missing and flipped concepts, supported uncertainty-aware intervention, and extracted compact task-relevant concept subsets without degrading downstream performance.

</details>


### [64] [Automating and Scaling Behavioral Scientific Research on AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10030)
*Soo Yong Lee,Jongha Lee,Jaewan Chun,Hyunjin Hwang,Fanchen Bu,Ziv Ben-Zion,Taekwan Kim,Denny Borsboom,Jaemin Yoo,Kijung Shin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AEROBAT 是首个可自动完成 AI 代理行为科学研究全流程的多智能体系统，针对 12 种目标行为生成并检验 79 条假设，设计 1240 场对照实验，执行 23512 次仿真，发现 26 条具有中到强统计证据的假设，验证了自动化研究的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 AI 代理在复杂环境中的广泛部署，理解其行为至关重要，但现有行为科学研究高度依赖人工，耗时且难以规模化，亟需自动化手段来提升研究效率和覆盖范围。

Method: 提出 AEROBAT 系统，接受用户指定的目标行为后，自动完成：① 生成行为假设；② 设计受控实验（包括变量控制和实验组/对照组设置）；③ 执行实验并收集仿真数据；④ 进行行为评估与统计分析；⑤ 自动撰写研究报告。系统在 12 种目标行为上共生成 79 条假设，设计 1240 场实验，累计运行 23512 次仿真。

Result: 在 79 条假设中，有 26 条获得中到强的统计证据支持，其中包括若干新颖假设。实验展示了 AEROBAT 能够高效生成、验证假设并产出可解释的研究报告，显著降低了人工工作量。

Conclusion: 自动化的行为科学研究平台能够补充并扩展人工研究的能力，为 AI 代理行为分析提供可扩展、系统化的工具，展示了在复杂环境中进行大规模行为研究的可行路径。

Abstract: As AI agents are increasingly deployed in complex environments, understanding their behaviors becomes critical. Yet behavioral scientific research on AI agents remains manual and labor-intensive. We introduce AEROBAT, the first multi-agent system to automate behavioral scientific research on AI agents. Given an arbitrary target behavior by its user, AEROBAT automatically executes a full pipeline of behavioral scientific research---generating hypotheses about the behavior, designing and executing controlled experiments, making behavioral assessments, analyzing the results, and writing reports. For 12 target behaviors, we used AEROBAT to generate and test 79 hypotheses: designing 1,240 controlled experiments and executing 23,512 simulation rounds in total. Moderate-to-strong statistical evidence was found for 26 hypotheses, including some novel ones. In sum, our results demonstrate that automated behavioral scientific research on AI agents can complement and extend the reach of manual research.

</details>


### [65] [CHORUS: Complementary Experts for High-Coverage Testbench Stimulus Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10090)
*Hejia Zhang,Sheng Lu,Zhongming Yu,Chia-Tung Ho,Brucek Khailany,Jishen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CHORUS是一种后训练框架，通过利用多阶段监督微调产生的多样化检查点和密集奖励强化学习，将不同任务的专长合并（或进一步后训练），在仅4B参数模型上实现了88.0% Pass@1，显著超越671B的DeepSeek-R1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件验证中的高覆盖率测试平台刺激生成是芯片设计的重要任务，现有的监督微调到强化学习的流水线难以兼顾整体性能和任务级别的专长，需要更有效的学习信号和模型融合方法。

Method: CHORUS基于两点：① 通过分阶段监督微调得到行为多样的检查点；随后使用密集奖励强化学习将这些检查点转化为在整体性能相当但在不同任务上各有优势的专家模型。② 将这些互补的专家通过无训练的模型合并或进一步的后训练进行融合，得到单一的4B模型。

Result: 合并后的4B模型在CVDP-ECov基准上实现了88.0% Pass@1，较DeepSeek-R1（671B）提升13.5个百分点，展示了在参数规模远小于对手的情况下仍能取得领先的性能。

Conclusion: 通过利用多样化的SFT检查点和密集奖励RL的互补优势，CHORUS在硬件验证代码生成任务上实现了高效的模型融合和性能提升，证明后训练和模型合并是提升小模型能力的有效路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have advanced code generation, where executable feedback provides a more reliable learning signal than textual imitation alone. Hardware verification is an important application of code generation and accounts for a substantial fraction of modern chip design effort, with high-coverage testbench stimulus generation as a key task. We present CHORUS, a post-training framework that pushes performance beyond what a conventional supervised fine-tuning (SFT)-to-reinforcement learning (RL) pipeline achieves. CHORUS builds on two observations. First, staged SFT produces behaviorally diverse checkpoints, and dense-reward RL turns them into strong experts with comparable aggregate performance but distinct task-level strengths. Second, these complementary strengths can be exploited through either training-free model merging or further post-training to outperform the best individual expert. By consolidating the resulting specialists into a single 4B model, CHORUS achieves 88.0% Pass@1 on CVDP-ECov, outperforming DeepSeek-R1 (671B) by 13.5 percentage points.

</details>


### [66] [MESA:Task-Adaptive Multi-Structure Evidence Selection for Long-Horizon Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10108)
*Beidi Zhao,Yaoqi Chen,Yuru Feng,Menghao Li,Qianxi Zhang,Baotong Lu,Jianan Lu,Zhirui Wang,Xinjiang Wang,Shusen Xu,Zengzhong Li,Xiaoxiao Li,Qi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MESA通过动态选择并融合多结构记忆，实现在长时序任务中以更少证据提升回答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序智能体的轨迹包含上百步的推理、动作和观察，单一记忆结构要么信息不全，要么引入大量噪声，难以满足不同查询的证据需求。

Method: 构建五种互补的结构化记忆视图，对每个查询在这些视图中动态选择子集并融合，使用端到端答案反馈进行学习；在弱监督下采用带先验的搜索与UCB调度的束缚优化平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在AMA-Bench上，MESA相较最强基线提升8.5%准确率，并且使用的证据token比全结构方案少41%。

Conclusion: 结构级动态选择能够根据查询和任务需求灵活组合记忆，显著提升长时序代理的检索效率和答案质量。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents accumulate trajectories spanning hundreds of interleaved reasoning, action, and observation steps, where answering a query may depend on evidence buried far back in the history. External memory stores such trajectories as structured representations, yet each structure provides a distinct and incomplete view. Existing multi-memory systems either read a fixed set of structures for every query, inflating context and introducing noise, or route each query to a single structure, preventing the composition of complementary evidence. A controlled analysis on AMA-Bench shows that the optimal memory configuration is typically neither a single structure nor the full union, but a tailored composition of multiple structural memories that varies with query and task demands. Motivated by these findings, we formulate structure-level dynamic selection: selecting and fusing a query-adaptive subset from a library of specialized memory structures. We propose MESA (a Multi-structure Evidence Selection framework for long-horizon Agent), which builds five complementary structure views of each trajectory and learns from end-to-end answer-level feedback to select and fuse a query-specific subset for a frozen answer model. To learn under this weak supervision, MESA employs harness optimization with prior-guided search and UCB-guided scheduling to balance exploration and exploitation. On AMA-Bench, MESA outperforms the strongest baseline by 8.5% while using 41% fewer evidence tokens than the all-structure alternative.

</details>


### [67] [The CASE Framework: A Multi-Disciplinary Control Architecture for Governing Enterprise Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10153)
*Srinivas Telukunta,Georgios Nektarios Lilis,Lucio Baron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CASE框架，将控制论、复杂适应系统、监督控制论和工程运维分别用于单体AI代理、代理集合、人机团队和代理舰队的治理，指出多层次治理的耦合难题并提出五级成熟度模型，以弥合“出现层风险”与实际治理能力之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业在快速部署自主AI代理的同时缺乏有效治理，现有的DevSecOps等单一学科方法无法覆盖多层次的代理系统，导致风险在出现层被忽视。

Method: 构建CASE框架，将控制理论、复杂适应系统理论、监督控制论和工程运维分别映射到四个治理层面，形式化每层模型并推导跨层耦合条件；通过三项实证研究（生产故障分析、工具覆盖评估、公开部署成熟度评分）验证框架。

Result: 发现82%生产故障涉及多层路径，22种生态工具均未覆盖第二层（出现层），35个公开部署全部位于最低成熟度；提出“出现层缺口”概念并给出基于非补偿瓶颈加权指数的五级成熟度模型及评估工具。

Conclusion: 只有满足必要多样性（requisite variety）的架构才能实现有效的人类监督，CASE框架提供了科学的治理模型，帮助企业在欧盟AI法案等监管要求下实现真实的人机监督。

Abstract: Enterprises are deploying autonomous AI agents faster than they can govern them, and prevailing approaches stretch a single discipline, typically DevSecOps built for deterministic automation, across every scale of agency. We argue that agentic AI governance is four problems, not one, each with a mature governing science. The CASE framework assigns Control theory to the individual agent (intent as setpoint, guardrails as feedback, evaluation as observation), complex Adaptive systems theory to agent collectives (where emergence makes single-agent assurance non-compositional), Supervisory cybernetics to human-agent teams (where the Law of Requisite Variety shows unaided human oversight fails structurally), and Engineering operations to fleets (extending error budgets to decision quality so autonomy becomes a controlled variable). We formalize each layer, derive cross-layer coupling conditions, including a zero-touch deployment paradox where excellence at one-layer strains the others, and trace twenty-plus enterprise controls to their classical constructs. Three empirical studies validate the thesis: 82 percent of documented production agent failures are multi-layer trajectories; none of 22 ecosystem tools offers full Layer 2 (emergence) coverage; and all 35 scored public deployments fall in the lowest maturity band. We name this mismatch, risk realized at the emergence layer against capability barely offered and practice absent, the Emergence Gap. A five-level maturity model with a non-compensatory bottleneck-weighted index and assessment instrument operationalizes CASE as a scientific rather than process maturity model, grounded in production enterprise agentic platforms. As EU AI Act Article 14 makes effective human oversight a legal requirement, only architectures satisfying requisite variety can make oversight real rather than ceremonial.

</details>


### [68] [SBCO: Self-Supervised, Verifier-Grounded Harness Optimization For Planning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10157)
*Vivek Kulkarni,Sudipta Paul,Aounon Kumar,Nicholas Tzou,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自监督块坐标优化器（SBCO），在无需自指或人工标签的情况下，通过验证器驱动的块坐标上升，显著降低计算成本并在两个任务上匹配或超越自修改基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自指式自改进方法（如Darwin Gödel Machine）仅在任务与自修改能力高度对齐时有效，且对不对齐任务需昂贵的搜索或元代理修改；因此需要一种计算更高效、适用于约束规划任务的自改进方案。

Method: SBCO 将自监督块坐标上升引入 Gödel‑machine 框架：在固定的元代理下，学习一组可分解的验证器和一个 harness 策略；通过近似块坐标上升交替优化验证器和策略，使代理依据自身的分级反馈自行改进输出。

Result: 在两个实验域中，SBCO 在计算预算仅为基线的 1/4‑1/5.5 的情况下，性能达到或超过了专门设计的自修改基线。

Conclusion: 自监督块坐标优化器提供了一种无需自指、无需人工标注且计算开销显著降低的自改进机制，展示了在约束规划任务中可行且高效的自我提升路径。

Abstract: Self-improving agents seek to reduce the human engineering effort behind AI systems by enabling them to evolve and self-improve their performance over time. Recently, methods like the Darwin Gödel Machine and the Huxley Gödel Machine have been proposed which enable open-ended, recursive self-improvement through self-reference where a coding agent edits its own code. Such self-referential self-improvement methods require that the competence required to perform the task coincides or aligns well with the competence required for self-modification which is the case for coding tasks. For domains or tasks, which do not satisfy the alignment needed, self-referential self-improvement is not available. In such cases, it is possible to adapt the above algorithms to other tasks by removing the self-referential aspect or introducing explicit self-modification of a meta-agent -- both computationally expensive, relying on population or self-modification search over many candidate agents. For planning tasks with explicit constraints, we propose a far cheaper alternative. We introduce SBCO (Self-supervised Block Coordinate Optimizer), a verifier-grounded harness optimizer in the same closed-loop, improve-from-experience family as the Gödel-machine methods, but self-supervised rather than self-referential. Given an agentic harness, SBCO learns a decomposed bank of verifiers and a harness policy via approximate block coordinate ascent, improving the agent's outputs from its own graded feedback---with a fixed meta-agent and no human labels. Across two domains SBCO matches or exceeds a customized self-modifying baseline while using 4-5.5 times less compute budget.

</details>


### [69] [Generating Attacks for LLMs with GFlowNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10171)
*Berkay Ozcam,Irem Onen,Mehmet Fatih Amasyali,Emin Islam Tatli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于 GFlowNet 的自动化红队攻击框架，用一个大模型攻击另一个大模型，实现人类独立、适应性强的安全评估，并在英文和土耳其文攻击上超越现有基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型广泛落地带来安全风险，现有红队评估要么依赖人工耗时，要么受限于固定攻击数据集缺乏创造性，亟需一种高效、自动且具创新性的漏洞发现方法。

Method: 构建攻击者模型并使用生成流网络（GFlowNet）进行训练，使其在给定受害模型的情况下生成多样化、有效的对抗输入；通过让一个大模型充当攻击者，对另一个大模型进行红队测试，并计算量化的鲁棒性得分；同时扩展至土耳其语攻击生成。

Result: 实验表明，该 GFlowNet 驱动的攻击在英文基准上产生的对抗样本比现有自动化攻击更具破坏性，显著降低受害模型的性能；此外，首次实现了能够生成土耳其语攻击输入的模型，展示了跨语言的攻击能力。

Conclusion: 基于 GFlowNet 的自动化红队方法能够在无需人工干预的情况下发现 LLM 漏洞，提升攻击多样性和效果，为大模型安全评估提供了更强的工具，并为多语言安全研究打开了新方向。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) has facilitated their ubiquitous integration into various domains, leading to widespread adoption. However, this escalating trend has introduced significant security vulnerabilities, necessitating the identification and mitigation of flaws arising from malicious exploitation. Red teaming assessments, conducted to evaluate model robustness through diverse adversarial inputs, are essential for exposing security risks and implementing countermeasures. Currently, red teaming is performed either manually by experts or automatically using predefined attack datasets. Nevertheless, manual testing remains time-consuming, while existing automated methods suffer from limited creativity due to their inherent dependency on fixed datasets. In this study, we propose an automated, human-independent, and adaptive approach leveraging GFlowNets to identify LLM vulnerabilities by utilizing one large language model to test another. Within this framework, an attacker model is trained against a specified victim model to perform automated red teaming and provide a quantitative robustness score. This research aims to generate more effective adversarial attacks in English compared to existing benchmarks and, as a novel contribution to the literature, introduces a model capable of generating attack inputs in the Turkish language.

</details>


### [70] [TRACE: Trustworthy Retrieval-Augmented Conversational Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10176)
*Touseef Hasan,Laila Cure,Souvika Sarkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于检索的约束感知对话框架TRACE，通过结构化约束解析和双重数据表示提升公共服务聊天机器人的推荐可靠性，检索质量提升显著降低幻觉并提升约束满足度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共服务目录噪声大、信息不一致，通用大语言模型在对话中常产生不可靠、未验证的推荐，难以满足用户显式约束，亟需提升检索质量以保证推荐可信。

Method: 设计TRACE框架：将用户查询解析为结构化和语义约束，利用双重数据表示（包括知识图谱）进行检索；在州级食品储备目录和合成查询基准上评估不同知识表示方式，比较多种开源LLM和专有模型的表现。

Result: 实验表明，强化检索显著提升用户约束满足率并大幅降低幻觉推荐；在检索质量提升后，各模型间性能差距缩小，对模型规模不敏感。不同知识表示（有无KG）对效果亦有影响。

Conclusion: 检索质量是构建稳健公共服务对话系统的关键因素，采用约束感知的检索增强框架能够在噪声目录环境下实现可靠推荐，降低对大模型规模的依赖。

Abstract: Public service chatbots are expected to deliver recommendations from an underlying public service directory, while also making sure that the recommendations respect explicit user constraints. In practice, public service directories are noisy and inconsistent, and general-purpose large language model (LLM) or AI-based chatbots frequently generate unreliable recommendations, citing unverified sources from the web. We investigate the impact of retrieval quality on constraint-aware recommendation in public service conversational systems built over noisy and heterogeneous service directories. We propose TRACE (Trustworthy Retrieval-Augmented Conversational Engine), a retrieval-based, constraint-aware framework that parses input user queries into structural and semantic constraints for downstream retrieval, with the help of a dual data representation schema. Using a curated statewide pantry directory and a synthetic query benchmark, we evaluate multiple knowledge-representation variants with and without knowledge graphs (KGs). We experiment with several open-source LLMs and a proprietary model, showing that strengthening retrieval substantially improves user constraint satisfaction while reducing hallucinated recommendations. Performance differences across LLMs narrowed in our experiments as retrieval quality improved, making results less sensitive to model size. These findings suggest that the quality of retrieval is key for robust public service conversational systems.

</details>


### [71] [Post-Hoc Sparse Coding of Latent Communication Between Vision-Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10198)
*Di Wu,Xiaohui Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在冻结的 Vision Wormhole 激活上后置稀疏自编码器，作者发现仅用每个 token 4 个系数的 uint16‑index/float16‑value 稀疏表示即可将传输字节压缩 128 倍，且在七个推理任务上的平均准确率几乎不变（49.85%→49.77%），表明潜在空间通信具有很高的可压缩性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Wormhole 将视觉特征直接映射为统一的潜在张量进行跨模型通信，但每条消息始终以固定大小的密集张量传输，导致大量未被利用的表示自由度。作者希望探究该通信通道的冗余程度，并评估是否可以在不显著损失性能的前提下进行压缩。

Method: 在冻结的 Vision Wormhole 激活上训练后置稀疏自编码器（SAE），使用 4096‑维字典并限制每个 token 仅保留 k=4 个活跃系数（uint16 索引 + float16 值）。通过重建误差、下游任务效用、特征复用率以及 token 级干预等指标，在九个推理基准上评估压缩效果。

Result: 稀疏负载将传输字节降低约 128 倍；七任务非 AIME 平均准确率从 49.85% 降至 49.77%，变化极小。字典实际只使用 50 个特征，任务级活跃集合的平均 Jaccard 相似度为 0.906，显示高度的特征共享和冗余。

Conclusion: Vision Wormhole 的潜在空间通信具有显著的后置可压缩性，稀疏编码能够在保持几乎相同推理性能的情况下大幅降低带宽需求。未来工作应进一步区分稀疏编码、位置选择、低精度和低秩结构等因素的贡献，并设计能够自适应信息量的通信机制。

Abstract: Latent-space communication allows heterogeneous vision-language model agents to exchange continuous representations without serializing visual and reasoning states into text. Vision Wormhole realizes this approach by translating visual features into a universal latent representation that can be consumed by another model, but every message is transported as a dense tensor of the same size regardless of its content. A fixed-capacity dense tensor therefore need not have a fixed effective information density: some messages may use only a small fraction of the available representational degrees of freedom. This observation suggests that the communication channel may be substantially compressible. We study its redundancy by fitting a post-hoc sparse autoencoder to frozen Vision Wormhole activations and measuring reconstruction, downstream utility, feature reuse, and token-level interventions across nine reasoning benchmarks. Relative to the original float32 transport, a uint16-index/float16-value sparse payload with k=4 active coefficients per token reduces the transmitted bytes by 128x. In a single-run evaluation, the seven-task non-AIME mean accuracy changes from 49.85% to 49.77%. The fitted 4096-element dictionary uses only 50 features, and task-level active sets have a mean pairwise Jaccard similarity of 0.906. These measurements establish strong post-hoc compressibility relative to the original transport, but do not yet isolate the incremental contribution of sparse coding from position selection, reduced precision, low-rank structure, or SAE optimization effects. The results motivate matched-payload comparisons and communication mechanisms whose payload adapts to the information used by each message.

</details>


### [72] [Edge Phoneme Recognition for Children's Speech through Age-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10206)
*Matthew Arboleda,Ryan Arboleda,Sophie Haak,Sam Hjelmeset,Andrew Franck,Bingrui Yang,Jose Bustamante Ortiz,Yuanrong Shen,Joel Walsh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在轻量模型中同时预测学习者年龄和音素序列，使94M参数的模型在儿童语音音素检测任务上超越317M的WavLM Large模型，并接近参数量是其90倍的竞赛集成模型的性能，实现了可在手机端运行的PhonemeTrainer应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童语音的音素检测受限于训练数据稀缺和儿童语音的独特特性，亟需高效、轻量且能在边缘设备上运行的模型，以提升儿童ASR和发音辅助应用的效果和隐私合规性。

Method: 在一次音素检测竞赛中，训练一个94M参数的轻量模型，同时预测学习者的年龄标签和音素序列。通过多任务学习让年龄信息帮助模型捕捉儿童语音的年龄相关变异，从而提升音素检测精度。

Result: 该轻量模型在目标DrivenData数据集上性能超过了317M参数的WavLM Large模型，并且在字符错误率（CER）上仅比使用90倍参数的竞赛集成模型高约0.04，展示了极高的参数效率。

Conclusion: 利用年龄预测的多任务学习可显著提升儿童语音音素检测的效果，使得低参数模型能够在移动设备上实时运行，为儿童ASR和发音辅助提供了更好的隐私保护和合规性。

Abstract: Detecting phonemes from children's speech has historically been difficult due to the scarcity of training data, and unique characteristics of children's speech. During a phoneme detection competition, we found that training a lightweight model to predict the age of the learner, as well as the phoneme sequence, enabled a 94M-parameter model to outperform WavLM Large models (317M) on the target DrivenData distribution, and fall within approximately 0.04 CER of competition ensembles with 90 times the parameters. This has enabled the creation of PhonemeTrainer, an application that can run on most modern cellular phones. This will ultimately enable better Automated Speech Recognition (ASR) and pronunciation helper apps for children's speech, with the privacy and compliance benefits that come with edge processing.

</details>


### [73] [Mitigating Bus Bunching with Reinforcement Learning Enhanced by Semantic Stop Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10207)
*Xin Dong,Vikash V. Gayah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用离线大语言模型将公交站点的物理属性、周边活动和历史运营特征转化为语义嵌入，结合深度Q学习实现事件驱动的公交等候控制，在两条线路的仿真中显著降低车头间距波动、拥挤事件和乘客等候时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于强化学习的公交等候控制仅使用即时运营变量或线路特定的站点标识，信息量有限，导致难以在不同线路间复用策略，限制了控制效果的提升。

Method: 离线使用大语言模型（LLM）对异构站点信息（包括站点物理属性、周边活动上下文和历史运营特征）进行语义化编码，生成固定维度的嵌入向量；这些嵌入与常规运营状态一起输入深度Q学习控制器，实现事件驱动的等候决策，无需实时LLM推理。

Result: 在基于观测数据校准的两条公交线路的随机仿真中，语义控制器相较于最佳校准的Daganzo基线，分别将车头间距变异、拥挤事件和乘客等候时间降低了32.0%、69.2%和24.0%。路段特定标识对仅间距控制无提升，而语义信息提升了间距规律性、等候时间和等候力度的整体权衡。跨线路实验表明，零样本迁移的直接泛化有限，热启动微调加速了学习并提升了迁移策略，而从头训练仍能获得最佳最终性能。

Conclusion: 语义化的站点状态表征能够补充传统运营状态，提升公交等候控制的效果，并支持在相关线路之间的适应性策略复用，为基于强化学习的公共交通调度提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Bus bunching degrades service regularity and increases passenger waiting in high-frequency transit. Existing reinforcement-learning-based holding controllers primarily rely on instantaneous operational variables or route-specific stop identifiers, which provide limited information about the functional and operational context of individual stops and constrain policy reuse across routes. This study introduces an LLM-assisted semantic stop representation for event-driven bus holding control. An LLM is used offline to transform heterogeneous stop information, including physical attributes, surrounding activity context, and historical operational characteristics, into fixed semantic embeddings that are incorporated into a deep Q-learning controller without requiring real-time LLM inference. Experiments are conducted in stochastic simulations calibrated with observed data from two bus routes. Compared with the best calibrated Daganzo baseline, the semantic controller reduces headway variability, bunching events, and passenger waiting time by 32.0%, 69.2%, and 24.0%, respectively. A route-specific stop identifier does not improve the spacing-only controller, whereas semantic stop information improves headway regularity, waiting time, and holding effort, providing a more favorable overall trade-off across control objectives. Cross-route experiments further show that zero-shot transfer provides limited immediate generalization, while warm-start fine-tuning accelerates early-stage learning and improves transferred policies; cold-start training nevertheless achieves the best final performance. These findings suggest that semantic state representations can complement conventional operational states and support adaptation-based policy reuse across related transit routes.

</details>


### [74] [Evaluation-Conditioned Training: Teaching Models to Generalize to Stronger Oversight Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10209)
*Alec Harris,Kasey Corra,Archie Chaudhury,Yixiong Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出评估条件训练（ECT）框架，通过在训练样本中加入反馈可信度的自然语言条件，并在部署时使用高可信度监控器，引导模型在不完美反馈下实现更可靠的对齐行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后训练方法（如SFT、PPO）依赖的人类标注或自动奖励函数难以完整捕捉期望的反馈，导致奖励误规和对齐不足，尤其在反馈不完美的情境下表现受限。

Method: ECT 将每条训练样本用自然语言描述其反馈的可信度作为条件输入，训练时让模型学习在不同可信度下的行为；部署时通过高可信度的监控器对模型进行条件化，从而在实际使用中倾向于高质量反馈的行为。

Result: 在两个概念验证实验中：①新闻生成任务中提升客观性（even‑handedness），②算术任务中降低迎合用户的倾向（sycophancy），ECT 均相较于直接训练（SFT/PPO）显著提升了目标行为的表现。

Conclusion: ECT 作为一种可叠加于现有后训练算法的框架，有潜力缓解奖励误规问题，帮助 LLM 在面对不完美反馈时更好地对齐人类价值和目标。

Abstract: Feedback signals used to train Large Language Models (LLMs) are the primary driver of their behavior and our main lever for instilling alignment with human values and objectives. However, a key limitation of current post-training methods is the inability of human annotators and automated reward functions to faithfully capture the feedback we would like to give. We introduce Evaluation-Conditioned Training (ECT), a post-training framework that uses natural language to condition each training sample on the fidelity of the feedback we provide and then elicits the desired behavior by conditioning the LLM on a high-fidelity monitor in deployment.
